Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in surfaces theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 6 and the invariant I(A,B) = 399 mod 2.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on surfaces structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 6. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 399. QED.

Theorem 219.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the surfaces object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 665. The moduli space has dimension at most 777 with 5 connected components.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 665 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the surfaces structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 777. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
